4 | const apiBaseUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL ?? 'http://localhost:8000'
5 | 
6 | async function apiError(response: Response): Promise<Error> {
7 |   const payload: unknown = await response.json().catch(() => null)
8 |   const detail =
9 |     typeof payload === 'object' && payload !== null && 'detail' in payload && typeof payload.detail === 'string'
10 |       ? payload.detail
11 |       : `API returned ${response.status}`
12 |   return new Error(detail)
13 | }
14 | 
15 | export async function getHealth(signal: AbortSignal): Promise<HealthResponse> {
16 |   const response = await fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/api/health`, { signal })
17 |   if (!response.ok) {
18 |     throw new Error(`API returned ${response.status}`)
19 |   }
20 |   return response.json() as Promise<HealthResponse>
21 | }
22 | 
23 | export async function uploadProject(file: File): Promise<ProjectUploadResponse> {
24 |   const formData = new FormData()
25 |   formData.append('file', file)
26 | 
27 |   const response = await fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/api/projects`, {
28 |     method: 'POST',
29 |     body: formData,
30 |   })
31 | 
32 |   if (!response.ok) {
33 |     throw await apiError(response)
34 |   }
35 | 
36 |   return response.json() as Promise<ProjectUploadResponse>
37 | }
38 |

40 |   const response = await fetch(`${apiBaseUrl}/api/projects/${encodeURIComponent(projectId)}`)

Contents of file backend/app/services/project_storage.py:

1 | import os
2 | import re
3 | import shutil
4 | import tempfile
5 | import uuid
6 | from pathlib import Path
7 | 
8 | from fastapi import UploadFile
9 | from pydantic import ValidationError
10 | 
11 | from app.models.codebase import File, Project
12 | from app.models.project import ProjectUploadResponse
13 | from app.services.project_archive import IGNORED_DIRECTORIES, extract_project_archive
14 | 
15 | PROJECT_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[a-f0-9]{32}$")
16 | 
17 | 
18 | class ProjectNotFoundError(LookupError):
19 |     pass
20 | 
21 | 
22 | def _projects_root() -> Path:
23 |     configured_root = os.environ.get("GREPO_PROJECTS_DIR")
24 |     root = Path(configured_root) if configured_root else Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "grepo" / "projects"
25 |     return root.expanduser().resolve()
26 | 
27 | 
28 | def get_project_directory(project_id: str) -> Path:
29 |     if not PROJECT_ID_PATTERN.fullmatch(project_id):
30 |         raise ProjectNotFoundError("Project not found.")
31 | 
32 |     root = _projects_root()
33 |     project_directory = root / project_id
34 |     if project_directory.is_symlink() or not project_directory.is_dir():
35 |         raise ProjectNotFoundError("Project not found.")
36 |     return project_directory
37 | 
38 | 
39 | def get_project(project_id: str) -> Project:
40 |     project_directory = get_project_directory(project_id)
41 |     metadata_path = _projects_root() / f"{project_id}.json"
42 |     try:
43 |         project_metadata = Project.model_validate_json(metadata_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
44 |     except (OSError, ValidationError) as error:
45 |         raise ProjectNotFoundError("Project not found.") from error
46 |     if project_metadata.id != project_id:
47 |         raise ProjectNotFoundError("Project not found.")
48 |     return Project(
49 |         id=project_metadata.id,
50 |         name=project_metadata.name,
51 |         files=get_project_files_from_directory(project_directory),
52 |     )
53 | 
54 | 
55 | def _language_for_path(path: Path) -> str:
56 |     return {
57 |         ".py": "python",
58 |         ".js": "javascript",
59 |         ".jsx": "javascript",
60 |         ".mjs": "javascript",
61 |         ".ts": "typescript",
62 |         ".tsx": "typescript",
63 |         ".json": "json",
64 |         ".md": "markdown",
65 |         ".toml": "toml",
66 |         ".yaml": "yaml",
67 |         ".yml": "yaml",
68 |         ".html": "html",
69 |         ".css": "css",
70 |     }.get(path.suffix.lower(), "unknown")
71 | 
72 | 
73 | def get_project_files(project_id: str) -> list[File]:
74 |     return get_project_files_from_directory(get_project_directory(project_id))
75 | 
76 | 
77 | def get_project_files_from_directory(project_directory: Path) -> list[File]:
78 |     files: list[File] = []
79 |     for current_directory, directories, filenames in os.walk(project_directory, followlinks=False):
80 |         current_path = Path(current_directory)
81 |         directories[:] = sorted(
82 |             directory
83 |             for directory in directories
84 |             if directory.casefold() not in IGNORED_DIRECTORIES
85 |             and not (current_path / directory).is_symlink()
86 |         )
87 |         for filename in sorted(filenames):
88 |             source_path = current_path / filename
89 |             if source_path.is_symlink():
90 |                 continue
91 |             try:
92 |                 relative_path = source_path.resolve().relative_to(project_directory.resolve()).as_posix()
93 |                 size = source_path.stat().st_size
94 |             except (OSError, ValueError):
95 |                 continue
96 |             files.append(
97 |                 File(
98 |                     path=relative_path,
99 |                     name=source_path.name,
100 |                     language=_language_for_path(source_path),
101 |                     size=size,
102 |                 )
103 |             )
104 |     return files
105 | 
106 | 
107 | def _project_name(filename: str | None) -> str:
108 |     archive_name = (filename or "").replace("\\", "/").rsplit("/", maxsplit=1)[-1]
109 |     if archive_name.lower().endswith(".zip"):
110 |         archive_name = archive_name[:-4]
111 |     return archive_name.strip()[:100] or "Untitled project"
112 | 
113 | 
114 | async def store_project_archive(upload: UploadFile) -> ProjectUploadResponse:
115 |     root = _projects_root()
116 |     root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
117 |     project_id = uuid.uuid4().hex
118 |     staging_directory = root / f".{project_id}.staging"
119 |     extraction_root = staging_directory / "files"
120 |     archive_path = staging_directory / "upload.zip"
121 |     metadata_staging_path = staging_directory / "project.json"
122 |     project_directory = root / project_id
123 |     metadata_path = root / f"{project_id}.json"
124 |     project_name = _project_name(upload.filename)
125 |     staging_directory.mkdir()
126 |     directory_published = False
127 |     metadata_published = False
128 |     complete = False
129 | 
130 |     try:
131 |         file_count = await extract_project_archive(upload, archive_path, extraction_root)
132 | 
133 |         archive_path.unlink()
134 |         metadata_staging_path.write_text(
135 |             Project(id=project_id, name=project_name).model_dump_json(exclude={"files"}),
136 |             encoding="utf-8",
137 |         )
138 |         extraction_root.replace(project_directory)
139 |         directory_published = True
140 |         metadata_staging_path.replace(metadata_path)
141 |         metadata_published = True
142 |         response = ProjectUploadResponse(project_id=project_id, name=project_name, file_count=file_count)
143 |         complete = True
144 |         return response
145 |     finally:
146 |         if not complete:
147 |             if metadata_published:
148 |                 metadata_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
149 |             if directory_published:
150 |                 shutil.rmtree(project_directory, ignore_errors=True)
151 |         if staging_directory.exists():
152 |             shutil.rmtree(staging_directory, ignore_errors=True)

Contents of file backend/app/services/project_analysis.py:

1 | import os
2 | import tokenize
3 | from pathlib import Path
4 | 
5 | from app.core.analyzers import analyzer_service
6 | from app.models.codebase import File
7 | from app.services.project_archive import IGNORED_DIRECTORIES
8 | from app.services.project_storage import (
9 |     ProjectNotFoundError,
10 |     get_project_directory,
11 | )
12 | 
13 | 
14 | class ProjectAnalysisError(ValueError):
15 |     pass
16 | 
17 | 
18 | def analyze_project_files(project_id: str) -> list[File]:
19 |     project_directory = get_project_directory(project_id)
20 |     resolved_project_directory = project_directory.resolve()
21 |     results: list[File] = []
22 | 
23 |     for current_directory, directories, filenames in os.walk(project_directory, followlinks=False):
24 |         current_path = Path(current_directory)
25 |         directories[:] = sorted(
26 |             directory
27 |             for directory in directories
28 |             if directory.casefold() not in IGNORED_DIRECTORIES
29 |             and not (current_path / directory).is_symlink()
30 |         )
31 | 
32 |         for filename in sorted(filenames):
33 |             source_path = current_path / filename
34 |             if source_path.is_symlink() or source_path.suffix.lower() != ".py":
35 |                 continue
36 | 
37 |             resolved_source_path = source_path.resolve()
38 |             try:
39 |                 relative_path = resolved_source_path.relative_to(resolved_project_directory)
40 |             except ValueError:
41 |                 continue
42 | 
43 |             relative_path_text = relative_path.as_posix()
44 |             try:
45 |                 with tokenize.open(source_path) as source_file:
46 |                     source = source_file.read()
47 |             except (SyntaxError, UnicodeDecodeError, LookupError) as error:
48 |                 raise ProjectAnalysisError(f"Could not read Python source at '{relative_path_text}'.") from error
49 | 
50 |             try:
51 |                 results.append(
52 |                     analyzer_service.analyze(
53 |                         relative_path_text,
54 |                         source,
55 |                         size=source_path.stat().st_size,
56 |                     )
57 |                 )
58 |             except SyntaxError as error:
59 |                 raise ProjectAnalysisError(f"Could not parse Python source at '{relative_path_text}'.") from error
60 | 
61 |     return results

Contents of file backend/app/services/project_archive.py:

1 | import stat
2 | import zipfile
3 | import zlib
4 | from dataclasses import dataclass
5 | from pathlib import Path, PureWindowsPath
6 | 
7 | from fastapi import UploadFile
8 | 
9 | MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024
10 | MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES = 10_000
11 | MAX_FILE_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024
12 | MAX_TOTAL_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES = 250 * 1024 * 1024
13 | CHUNK_SIZE = 64 * 1024
14 | IGNORED_DIRECTORIES = {".git", "node_modules", "__pycache__", ".venv", "dist", "build"}
15 | WINDOWS_RESERVED_NAMES = {
16 |     "CON", "PRN", "AUX", "NUL",
17 |     *(f"COM{index}" for index in range(1, 10)),
18 |     *(f"LPT{index}" for index in range(1, 10)),
19 | }
20 | WINDOWS_INVALID_CHARACTERS = '<>:"|?*'
21 | 
22 | 
23 | class ProjectUploadError(ValueError):
24 |     pass
25 | 
26 | 
27 | class ProjectUploadTooLargeError(ProjectUploadError):
28 |     pass
29 | 
30 | 
31 | @dataclass(frozen=True)
32 | class ArchiveEntry:
33 |     info: zipfile.ZipInfo
34 |     relative_path: Path
35 |     path_parts: tuple[str, ...]
36 |     is_directory: bool
37 | 
38 | 
39 | def _member_path(info: zipfile.ZipInfo) -> tuple[Path, tuple[str, ...], bool]:
40 |     name = info.filename
41 |     if not name or "\x00" in name or any(ord(character) < 32 for character in name):
42 |         raise ProjectUploadError("The archive contains an invalid path.")
43 | 
44 |     normalized_name = name.replace("\\", "/")
45 |     has_trailing_separator = normalized_name.endswith("/")
46 |     if has_trailing_separator:
47 |         normalized_name = normalized_name[:-1]
48 | 
49 |     windows_path = PureWindowsPath(normalized_name)
50 |     if (
51 |         normalized_name.startswith("/")
52 |         or windows_path.is_absolute()
53 |         or windows_path.drive
54 |         or not normalized_name
55 |     ):
56 |         raise ProjectUploadError("The archive contains an absolute path.")
57 | 
58 |     parts = tuple(normalized_name.split("/"))
59 |     if any(part in ("", ".", "..") for part in parts):
60 |         raise ProjectUploadError("The archive contains a path traversal entry.")
61 |     for part in parts:
62 |         if any(character in WINDOWS_INVALID_CHARACTERS for character in part) or part.endswith((" ", ".")):
63 |             raise ProjectUploadError("The archive contains an unsafe path component.")
64 |         if part.split(".", maxsplit=1)[0].upper() in WINDOWS_RESERVED_NAMES:
65 |             raise ProjectUploadError("The archive contains a reserved path component.")
66 | 
67 |     unix_mode = info.external_attr >> 16
68 |     file_type = stat.S_IFMT(unix_mode)
69 |     if stat.S_ISLNK(unix_mode) or info.external_attr & 0xFFFF & 0x400:
70 |         raise ProjectUploadError("Symbolic links are not allowed in project archives.")
71 |     if file_type not in (0, stat.S_IFREG, stat.S_IFDIR):
72 |         raise ProjectUploadError("The archive contains a non-regular file.")
73 | 
74 |     is_directory = has_trailing_separator or info.is_dir() or file_type == stat.S_IFDIR
75 |     if file_type == stat.S_IFREG and is_directory:
76 |         raise ProjectUploadError("The archive contains a conflicting file type.")
77 | 
78 |     return Path(*parts), parts, is_directory
79 | 
80 | 
81 | def _validated_entries(archive: zipfile.ZipFile) -> list[ArchiveEntry]:
82 |     infos = archive.infolist()
83 |     if not infos:
84 |         raise ProjectUploadError("The ZIP archive is empty.")
85 |     if len(infos) > MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES:
86 |         raise ProjectUploadError(f"The archive may contain at most {MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES} entries.")
87 | 
88 |     entries: list[ArchiveEntry] = []
89 |     path_types: dict[str, bool] = {}
90 |     declared_uncompressed_bytes = 0
91 | 
92 |     for info in infos:
93 |         relative_path, parts, is_directory = _member_path(info)
94 |         if info.flag_bits & 0x1:
95 |             raise ProjectUploadError("Encrypted ZIP entries are not supported.")
96 |         if info.compress_type not in (zipfile.ZIP_STORED, zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED):
97 |             raise ProjectUploadError("The archive uses an unsupported compression method.")
98 |         directory_parts = parts if is_directory else parts[:-1]
99 |         if any(part.casefold() in IGNORED_DIRECTORIES for part in directory_parts):
100 |             continue
101 | 
102 |         path_key = "/".join(parts).casefold()
103 |         previous_type = path_types.get(path_key)
104 |         if previous_type is not None:
105 |             if previous_type and is_directory:
106 |                 continue
107 |             raise ProjectUploadError("The archive contains duplicate or conflicting paths.")
108 |         path_types[path_key] = is_directory
109 | 
110 |         if not is_directory:
111 |             if info.file_size > MAX_FILE_BYTES:
112 |                 raise ProjectUploadError(f"A file exceeds the {MAX_FILE_BYTES // (1024 * 1024)} MiB limit.")
113 |             declared_uncompressed_bytes += info.file_size
114 |             if declared_uncompressed_bytes > MAX_TOTAL_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES:
115 |                 raise ProjectUploadError("The extracted project exceeds the total size limit.")
116 | 
117 |         entries.append(ArchiveEntry(info, relative_path, parts, is_directory))
118 | 
119 |     for entry in entries:
120 |         folded_parts = tuple(part.casefold() for part in entry.path_parts)
121 |         for index in range(1, len(folded_parts)):
122 |             parent_key = "/".join(folded_parts[:index])
123 |             if path_types.get(parent_key) is False:
124 |                 raise ProjectUploadError("A file path conflicts with a directory path in the archive.")
125 | 
126 |     if not any(not entry.is_directory for entry in entries):
127 |         raise ProjectUploadError("The archive contains no uploadable files.")
128 |     return entries
129 | 
130 | 
131 | async def _save_upload(upload: UploadFile, archive_path: Path) -> None:
132 |     if upload.size is not None and upload.size > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
133 |         raise ProjectUploadTooLargeError("The ZIP upload exceeds the 50 MiB limit.")
134 | 
135 |     size = 0
136 |     with archive_path.open("xb") as destination:
137 |         while chunk := await upload.read(CHUNK_SIZE):
138 |             size += len(chunk)
139 |             if size > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES:
140 |                 raise ProjectUploadTooLargeError("The ZIP upload exceeds the 50 MiB limit.")
141 |             destination.write(chunk)
142 | 
143 | 
144 | def _extract_entries(
145 |     archive: zipfile.ZipFile,
146 |     entries: list[ArchiveEntry],
147 |     extraction_root: Path,
148 | ) -> int:
149 |     extraction_root.mkdir()
150 |     resolved_root = extraction_root.resolve()
151 |     extracted_bytes = 0
152 |     file_count = 0
153 | 
154 |     for entry in entries:
155 |         destination = (extraction_root / entry.relative_path).resolve()
156 |         try:
157 |             destination.relative_to(resolved_root)
158 |         except ValueError as error:
159 |             raise ProjectUploadError("The archive contains a path outside the project directory.") from error
160 | 
161 |         if entry.is_directory:
162 |             destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
163 |             continue
164 | 
165 |         destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
166 |         file_bytes = 0
167 |         with archive.open(entry.info, "r") as source, destination.open("xb") as output:
168 |             while chunk := source.read(CHUNK_SIZE):
169 |                 file_bytes += len(chunk)
170 |                 extracted_bytes += len(chunk)
171 |                 if file_bytes > MAX_FILE_BYTES or extracted_bytes > MAX_TOTAL_UNCOMPRESSED_BYTES:
172 |                     raise ProjectUploadError("The extracted project exceeds the size limit.")
173 |                 if file_bytes > entry.info.file_size:
174 |                     raise ProjectUploadError("An archive entry expands beyond its declared size.")
175 |                 output.write(chunk)
176 | 
177 |         if file_bytes != entry.info.file_size:
178 |             raise ProjectUploadError("An archive entry does not match its declared size.")
179 |         file_count += 1
180 | 
181 |     return file_count
182 | 
183 | 
184 | async def extract_project_archive(
185 |     upload: UploadFile,
186 |     archive_path: Path,
187 |     extraction_root: Path,
188 | ) -> int:
189 |     await _save_upload(upload, archive_path)
190 |     try:
191 |         with zipfile.ZipFile(archive_path, "r") as archive:
192 |             entries = _validated_entries(archive)
193 |             return _extract_entries(archive, entries, extraction_root)
194 |     except (
195 |         zipfile.BadZipFile,
196 |         zipfile.LargeZipFile,
197 |         RuntimeError,
198 |         NotImplementedError,
199 |         EOFError,
200 |         zlib.error,
201 |     ) as error:
202 |         raise ProjectUploadError("The uploaded file is not a valid supported ZIP archive.") from error

4 | class Function(BaseModel):
5 |     name: str
6 |     file: str
7 |     line_start: int
8 |     line_end: int

23 |     functions: list[Function] = Field(default_factory=list)

6 | ResultT = TypeVar("ResultT", bound=BaseModel, covariant=True)
7 | 
8 | 
9 | class FeatureService(Protocol[RequestT, ResultT]):
10 |     async def execute(self, request: RequestT) -> ResultT:
11 |         """Execute a feature request and return its structured result."""

---044 tool---
Contents of file .grepo-reference/Grepo_PRD_Package/Grepo_Master_PRD.md:

1150 | The user owns shared UI/visualization direction. Each feature contributor owns their backend behavior and frontend behavior inside that shared shell. Assign one integration owner for canonical IDs, contracts and fixtures; otherwise the likely failure is six independently shaped responses that cannot share a map.
1151 | 
1152 | Use feature branches or short-lived changes with contract tests. Each change links requirement IDs, adds a positive example and a counterexample, and states whether it changes an API or evidence schema. A shared integration checklist is more useful than a long role document.
1153 | 
1154 | ## 29.3 Required work packages
1155 | 
1156 | **Foundation:** source adapter, snapshot store, workspace authorization, run lifecycle, capability registry, schema definitions and typed client. **Analysis:** parser adapters, resolvers, findings and retrieval. **Interface:** native shell, tree/source viewer, Archify projection/bridge and state handling. **Intelligence:** Watson adapter, prompts, output validation and cost ledger. **Handoff:** proposals, README, offline export and Bob evidence tools. **Verification:** fixtures, security tests, evaluation results and visual regression.
1157 | 
1158 | Every work package must leave a usable artifact. A renderer integration is not complete because an HTML file exists; it needs failure handling, verified source mapping and user interaction. A model integration is not complete because one prompt returns a string; it needs configured limits, grounding and error behavior.
1159 | 
1160 | # 30. Business value and product learning plan
1161 | 
1162 | ## 30.1 High-priority problem and value hypothesis
1163 | 
1164 | The problem chosen by the user is concrete: a developer must understand unfamiliar code well enough to make a change. Grepo’s value hypothesis is that source-linked structure, relevant reading paths and reusable-code discovery reduce the effort needed to choose a correct starting point. This document does not invent market size, customer logos or financial impact data.
1165 | 
1166 | A practical initial use case is open-source onboarding or small-team project handoff. An exportable evidence artifact can be attached to an issue or shared before a contributor starts implementation. The maintainer remains responsible for deciding whether a proposed change is appropriate.
1167 | 
1168 | ## 30.2 Product value tests
1169 | 
1170 | Ask users to complete a real location/change task, not just rate whether the interface is attractive. Capture whether the identified file was correct, whether the user inspected the evidence, whether a missed boundary changed their plan, and whether an existing helper prevented unnecessary new code. Measure the exported handoff’s usefulness by whether another developer can explain the same plan without repeating the entire exploration.
1171 | 
1172 | A simple estimated value model is time saved per completed task multiplied by task frequency and a user-supplied cost-of-time assumption. Do not insert an unsupported developer hourly rate or claim that every minute saved converts directly to revenue. Record assumptions and distinguish perceived convenience from measured correctness.
1173 | 
1174 | ## 30.3 Future commercial options, not hackathon distractions
1175 | 
1176 | Possible later packaging includes local/private workspaces, authenticated team sharing, larger-repository limits and recurring architecture snapshots. No pricing page or billing implementation is required for P0. The strongest evidence for business value is a repeated successful task, not a speculative subscription screen.
1177 | 
1178 | Do not present the tool as a substitute for security review, testing or an engineer’s judgment. The product should increase informed confidence, not confidence detached from evidence.
1179 | 
1180 | # 31. Risk register, unresolved choices and decision rules
1181 | 
1182 | | Risk / unresolved item | Decision rule and mitigation |
1183 | | --- | --- |
1184 | | “All languages” interpreted as universal semantic certainty | Admit all safe text; expose capabilities per feature; never turn missing capability into a negative finding. |
1185 | | Existing starter advances after inspection | Compare current checkout with pinned baseline before editing; preserve newer work. |
1186 | | Watson model not selected | Keep adapter/config generic; run a capability smoke test before enabling inference. |
1187 | | Budget prices unknown | Paid calls disabled until prices/limits are configured; use conservative reservations. |
1188 | | Archify update changes APIs or output | Pin revision; keep wrapper small; review updates separately and rerun renderer/bridge tests. |
1189 | | Overwhelming maps | Bounded projections, default small groups and progressive drill-down. |
1190 | | Generated mockups contain false product claims | Written correction table overrides text; source facts and accessibility take precedence. |
1191 | | High duplicate/unused false positives | Minimum size thresholds, entry-point rules, counterexamples and review-only language. |
1192 | | Prompt injection via repository docs | Fixed instruction hierarchy, tool allowlists, source-as-data envelope and adversarial tests. |
1193 | | Share/export leaks code | Explicit excerpt policy, redaction, no automatic publication and offline artifact review. |
1194 | | Multi-feature integration drifts | Canonical contracts, stable IDs and one integration owner. |
1195 | | User trusts a graph as runtime proof | Distinguish authored/resolved relations, inference and runtime observations. |
1196 | | Impressive but nonfunctional demo | Label fixtures/caches, remove placeholder data from live mode and complete G7. |
1197 | | Grepo license not selected | Project owner chooses before distribution; preserve all upstream notices regardless. |
1198 | 
1199 | The inspected README says a project license has not yet been selected. This PRD does not select one on the owner’s behalf. Archify’s own licensing/attribution obligations remain separate. [S03, S09]
1200 | 
1201 | Outstanding choices that do not block this specification: exact Watson model, hosting provider, which parser package builds pass in the team environment, actual Bob version for final acceptance and final repository license. Do not reopen already locked product intent, branding, read-only behavior or budget simply because these implementation choices remain configurable.
1202 | 
1203 | # 32. Implementation-agent instructions and handoff
1204 | 
1205 | ## 32.1 Read this before writing code
1206 | 
1207 | Read the current repository and compare it with the inspected baseline. Read the exact Archify skill and the schema/example for the diagram type being implemented. Use the bundled source reference map, not a guessed similarly named repository. Preserve the existing starter architecture and source ownership.
1208 | 
1209 | Implement one coherent requirement slice at a time across backend, frontend and tests. Use the contract schemas as design inputs, but reconcile them with the actual server-generated OpenAPI definition. Keep generated UI text and real code facts separate. Never populate live screens with the fabricated metrics or auth paths in the visual references.
1210 | 
1211 | For a change-related feature, include counterexamples and evidence limitations. For a source-reading feature, include authorization and path/range checks. For a model feature, include budget, timeout and output validation. For an export feature, include offline and injection tests. For a Bob integration, include actual session evidence and revision checks.
1212 | 
1213 | ## 32.2 Required completion report
1214 | 
1215 | Report requirement IDs implemented, files changed, contracts changed, commands actually executed, test outcomes, visual verification performed, unresolved issues and the final commit. Do not state “all tests pass” unless the relevant complete test command ran successfully on that final state. Do not state that screenshots were inspected unless they were actually reviewed.
1216 | 
1217 | An implementation agent must not auto-commit, push, publish, purchase services or expose private source merely because this PRD describes those future workflows. External actions require the user’s normal authorization. The product itself remains read-only with respect to analyzed repositories.
1218 | 
1219 | ## 32.3 Suggested Grepo README structure
1220 | 
1221 | Project promise; current implementation status; short getting-started commands verified from the actual repo; architecture and data flow; six-feature capabilities with honest coverage; IBM Bob development/handoff integration; Watson setup without credentials; import limits and privacy; test commands and fixture scope; screenshots labeled as mockup or implemented UI; limitations; attribution to Archify; and the owner-selected license.
1222 | 
1223 | The README should distinguish what is working today from planned enhancements. The same truth boundary applies to the hackathon submission. A compelling product does not need invented evidence.
1224 | 
1225 | # 33. Final release acceptance checklist
1226 | 
1227 | The release is acceptable only when all original core features have a live, non-placeholder path; both public GitHub and ZIP ingestion are safe; any-language text admission works; the evidence graph is consistent; source links are snapshot-correct; and the user can complete Connect → Understand → Plan without unexplained dead ends.
1228 | 
1229 | All paid model calls must respect configured caps. No credentials appear in frontend assets, prompts, logs or exports. No source execution, automatic refactor, deletion, commit or public publication is exposed by the app. The source/privacy notice matches actual behavior.
1230 | 
1231 | Archify integration must use the pinned source and preserve attribution. A final exported artifact must have matching provenance and deterministic validation evidence; browser behavior and perceptual review must be recorded separately. The application must not claim runtime certainty from graph reachability.
1232 | 
1233 | The interface must use the gecko/citrus/marble design direction while correcting misleading mockup content. Keyboard access, contrast, small-view containment, partial-state handling and offline export must pass. The Bob Change Pack must be readable, revision-aware and demonstrated through the installed Bob environment before claiming that handoff is complete.
1234 | 
1235 | The final product evidence should let a reviewer answer four questions: **Does it work end to end? Is IBM’s technology meaningfully integrated? Does it help a developer make a better-informed change? Is its distinctive experience backed by real engineering?**
1236 | 
1237 | # 34. Source register and inspection provenance
1238 | 
1239 | All external references below were consulted on 26 September 2026 unless otherwise stated. Repository source facts are pinned where possible. Product requirements, numeric targets and architecture choices are proposals in this document, not statements that the linked projects already implement them.

Truncate if needed\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    total_entities = len(entities)\n    truncated = total_entities > max_entities\n    if truncated:\n        entities = entities[:max_entities]\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 4. Build contains relations\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    relations: list[GraphRelation] = []\n    entity_ids = {e.id for e in entities}\n\n    for ent in entities:\n        if ent.parent_id and ent.parent_id in entity_ids:\n            rel_id = _stable_id(snapshot_id, f\"contains:{ent.parent_id}:{ent.id}\")\n            relations.append(GraphRelation(\n                id=rel_id,\n                source_id=ent.parent_id,\n                target_id=ent.id,\n                kind=RelationKind.CONTAINS,\n                basis=RelationBasis.OBSERVED,\n                evidence_ids=[],\n            ))\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # 5. Coverage\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    parsed_count = sum(1 for r in page.files if r.language == \"python\")\n    coverage = GraphCoverage(\n        inventoried_files=page.total,\n        parsed_files=parsed_count,\n        unresolved_references=0,\n        limitations=[\n            \"Only structural containment edges are shown. \"\n            \"Dependency/import edges are provided by the Dependencies integration slot.\"\n        ],\n        truncated=truncated,\n        total_entities=total_entities,\n        returned_entities=len(entities),\n    )\n\n    return Graph(\n        snapshot_id=snapshot_id,\n        entities=entities,\n        relations=relations,\n        coverage=coverage,\n    )\n",

INTEGRATION_SLOT\n * comments in source files are supplemental markers only.\n *\n * ── How to register a feature module (see INTEGRATION_GUIDE.md) ───────────\n *\n *   1. The corresponding workspace page will render your component.\n *\n * ── Contract ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n *\n *   - Unregistered slots render an IntegrationPlaceholder component.\n *   - Registration must not throw; failures are swallowed and the\n *     placeholder is shown instead.\n *   - A slot must not import private files from other feature modules.\n */\n\nimport type { ComponentType } from 'react'\nimport type { SelectedEntity, Snapshot } from '../contexts/WorkspaceContext'\nimport type { FileRecord } from '../types/v1'\n\n// ── Slot identifiers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport type SlotId =\n  | 'summaries.context-panel'\n  | 'dependencies.workspace'\n  | 'reuse.findings'\n  | 'duplicates.compare'\n  | 'unused.review'\n  | 'ask.workspace'\n  | 'proposals.detail'\n\n// ── Slot props (the public API a feature component receives) ───────────────\n\nexport interface SlotProps {\n  projectId: string\n  snapshotId: string\n  snapshot: Snapshot\n  selectedEntity: SelectedEntity | null\n  files: FileRecord[]\n}\n\n// ── Registry ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ntype SlotComponent = ComponentType<SlotProps>\n\nconst _registry = new Map<SlotId, SlotComponent>()\n\n/**\n * Register a feature component for a slot.\n *\n * Called by feature modules in their init file. Must not be called\n * from inside a React render tree.\n */\nexport function registerSlot(id: SlotId, component: SlotComponent): void {\n  _registry.set(id, component)\n}\n\n/**\n * Retrieve the registered component for a slot, or null if unregistered.\n *\n * Used by the workspace shell to decide whether to render a feature\n * component or the integration placeholder.\n */\nexport function getSlotComponent(id: SlotId): SlotComponent | null {\n  return _registry.get(id) ?? null\n}\n\n/**\n * Check whether a slot has been registered.\n * Useful in tests to verify registration without rendering.\n */\nexport function isSlotRegistered(id: SlotId): boolean {\n  return _registry.has(id)\n}\n\n/**\n * Return all registered slot IDs. Used in the developer disclosure panel.\n */\nexport function registeredSlots(): SlotId[] {\n  return Array.from(_registry.keys())\n}\n",

  "id": "tooluse_Yk2bYQT6ySKXKtfH7Juvuy",

    "content": "/**\n * StructureMap — bounded, readable SVG map built from actual repository\n * folders/files and structural containment.\n *\n * Features:\n *   - Bounded to 12 primary groups by default (PRD §9.3)\n *   - Pan and zoom via mouse/touch\n *   - Fit/zoom/reset controls\n *   - Selection sync with tree and source panel\n *   - Accessible list alternative (tab \"List view\")\n *   - Export trigger for the Archify-compiled HTML\n *\n * This map shows STRUCTURAL CONTAINMENT only (contains edges).\n * Dependency/import edges are NOT shown here; they come from the\n * Dependencies integration slot (INTEGRATION_SLOT: dependencies.workspace).\n *\n * No invented edges, no fictional topology, no animated background graph.\n */\n\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'\nimport type { Graph, GraphEntity } from '../../types/v1'\n\ninterface Props {\n  graph: Graph\n  selectedEntityId: string | null\n  onSelectEntity: (entity: GraphEntity) => void\n  onExport?: () => void\n}\n\n// ── Layout engine (simple tidy tree layout) ───────────────────────────────\n\ninterface LayoutNode {\n  entity: GraphEntity\n  x: number\n  y: number\n  width: number\n  height: number\n  children: LayoutNode[]\n  depth: number\n}\n\nconst NODE_W = 140\nconst NODE_H = 36\nconst H_GAP = 20\nconst V_GAP = 20\nconst PADDING = 40\n\nfunction buildLayoutTree(graph: Graph): LayoutNode[] {\n  const entityMap = new Map(graph.entities.map(e => [e.id, e]))\n  const childrenMap = new Map<string, string[]>()\n\n  for (const rel of graph.relations) {\n    if (rel.kind === 'contains') {\n      const list = childrenMap.get(rel.source_id) ?? []\n      list.push(rel.target_id)\n      childrenMap.set(rel.source_id, list)\n    }\n  }\n\n  const roots = graph.entities.filter(e => !e.parent_id || e.kind === 'repository')\n\n  let counter = { x: PADDING }\n\n  function buildNode(entity: GraphEntity, depth: number): LayoutNode {\n    const childIds = childrenMap.get(entity.id) ?? []\n    const children = childIds\n      .map(id => entityMap.get(id))\n      .filter((e): e is GraphEntity => !!e)\n      .map(e => buildNode(e, depth + 1))\n\n    const node: LayoutNode = {\n      entity,\n      x: 0,\n      y: PADDING + depth * (NODE_H + V_GAP),\n      width: NODE_W,\n      height: NODE_H,\n      children,\n      depth,\n    }\n\n    if (children.length === 0) {\n      node.x = counter.x\n      counter.x += NODE_W + H_GAP\n    } else {\n      const first = children[0]\n      const last = children[children.length - 1]\n      node.x = (first.x + last.x + last.width) / 2 - NODE_W / 2\n    }\n\n    return node\n  }\n\n  return roots.map(r => buildNode(r, 0))\n}\n\nfunction flattenLayout(nodes: LayoutNode[]): LayoutNode[] {\n  const result: LayoutNode[] = []\n  function visit(n: LayoutNode) {\n    result.push(n)\n    n.children.forEach(visit)\n  }\n  nodes.forEach(visit)\n  return result\n}\n\n// ── Camera / pan-zoom ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ninterface Camera {\n  x: number\n  y: number\n  scale: number\n}\n\n// ── Component ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ntype ViewMode = 'graph' | 'list'\n\nexport function StructureMap({ graph, selectedEntityId, onSelectEntity, onExport }: Props) {\n  const svgRef = useRef<SVGSVGElement>(null)\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)\n  const [camera, setCamera] = useState<Camera>({ x: 0, y: 0, scale: 1 })\n  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<ViewMode>('graph')\n  const isDragging = useRef(false)\n  const dragStart = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0, camX: 0, camY: 0 })\n\n  const roots = buildLayoutTree(graph)\n  const allNodes = flattenLayout(roots)\n\n  // Compute bounding box for fit-to-screen\n  const bounds = allNodes.reduce(\n    (acc, n) => ({\n      minX: Math.min(acc.minX, n.x),\n      minY: Math.min(acc.minY, n.y),\n      maxX: Math.max(acc.maxX, n.x + n.width),\n      maxY: Math.max(acc.maxY, n.y + n.height),\n    }),\n    { minX: Infinity, minY: Infinity, maxX: -Infinity, maxY: -Infinity },\n  )\n\n  const fitCamera = useCallback(() => {\n    const container = containerRef.current\n    if (!container || allNodes.length === 0) return\n    const { width, height } = container.getBoundingClientRect()\n    const contentW = bounds.maxX - bounds.minX + PADDING * 2\n    const contentH = bounds.maxY - bounds.minY + PADDING * 2\n    const scale = Math.min(1, width / contentW, height / contentH, 1.5)\n    setCamera({\n      x: (width - contentW * scale) / 2 - bounds.minX * scale + PADDING * scale,\n      y: (height - contentH * scale) / 2 - bounds.minY * scale + PADDING * scale,\n      scale,\n    })\n  }, [allNodes.length, bounds.maxX, bounds.maxY, bounds.minX, bounds.minY])\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    fitCamera()\n  }, [fitCamera])\n\n  // Mouse pan\n  function onMouseDown(e: React.MouseEvent) {\n    if (e.button !== 0) return\n    isDragging.current = true\n    dragStart.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, camX: camera.x, camY: camera.y }\n  }\n\n  function onMouseMove(e: React.MouseEvent) {\n    if (!isDragging.current) return\n    setCamera(c => ({\n      ...c,\n      x: dragStart.current.camX + (e.clientX - dragStart.current.x),\n      y: dragStart.current.camY + (e.clientY - dragStart.current.y),\n    }))\n  }\n\n  function onMouseUp() {\n    isDragging.current = false\n  }\n\n  // Wheel zoom\n  function onWheel(e: React.WheelEvent) {\n    e.preventDefault()\n    const container = containerRef.current\n    if (!container) return\n    const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect()\n    const mx = e.clientX - rect.left\n    const my = e.clientY - rect.top\n    const factor = e.deltaY < 0 ? 1.12 : 1 / 1.12\n    setCamera(c => {\n      const scale = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(4, c.scale * factor))\n      return {\n        scale,\n        x: mx - (mx - c.x) * (scale / c.scale),\n        y: my - (my - c.y) * (scale / c.scale),\n      }\n    })\n  }\n\n  function zoomIn() { setCamera(c => ({ ...c, scale: Math.min(4, c.scale * 1.25) })) }\n  function zoomOut() { setCamera(c => ({ ...c, scale: Math.max(0.2, c.scale / 1.25) })) }\n  function resetZoom() { setCamera({ x: 0, y: 0, scale: 1 }) }\n\n  // Node color\n  function nodeClass(entity: GraphEntity) {\n    const base = `map-node map-node--${entity.kind}`\n    const sel = entity.id === selectedEntityId ? ' map-node--selected' : ''\n    return base + sel\n  }\n\n  // Render contains edges\n  const edges: Array<{ x1: number; y1: number; x2: number; y2: number; id: string }> = []\n  for (const rel of graph.relations) {\n    if (rel.kind !== 'contains') continue\n    const src = allNodes.find(n => n.entity.id === rel.source_id)\n    const tgt = allNodes.find(n => n.entity.id === rel.target_id)\n    if (!src || !tgt) continue\n    edges.push({\n      id: rel.id,\n      x1: src.x + src.width / 2,\n      y1: src.y + src.height,\n      x2: tgt.x + tgt.width / 2,\n      y2: tgt.y,\n    })\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"map-canvas\" aria-label=\"Repository structure map\">\n      <div className=\"map-toolbar\" role=\"toolbar\" aria-label=\"Map controls\">\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          className={`btn btn--sm ${viewMode === 'graph' ? 'btn--secondary' : 'btn--ghost'}`}\n          onClick={() => setViewMode('graph')}\n          aria-pressed={viewMode === 'graph'}\n        >\n          Graph\n        </button>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          className={`btn btn--sm ${viewMode === 'list' ? This client-side module provides:\n *     1. A lightweight fallback export when the server-side Archify\n *        renderer is not yet configured (structural HTML only).\n *     2. `${snapshot.source.owner ?? ''}/${snapshot.source.repo}`\n      : `Snapshot ${snapshot.id.slice(0, 8)}`,\n  )\n\n  const revisionLabel = escHtml(\n    snapshot.source.resolved_commit\n      ? snapshot.source.resolved_commit.slice(0, 12)\n      : snapshot.source.archive_digest?.slice(0, 12) ?? []\n      list.push(rel.target_id)\n      childrenMap.set(rel.source_id, list)\n    }\n  }\n\n  interface SvgNode {\n    id: string\n    label: string\n    kind: string\n    x: number\n    y: number\n    depth: number\n    children: SvgNode[]\n  }\n\n  let xCounter = PADDING\n  function buildSvgNode(entityId: string, depth: number): SvgNode {\n    const entity = entityMap.get(entityId)\n    const label = entity?.label ?? entityId.slice(0, 12)\n    const kind = entity?.kind ?? 'file'\n    const childIds = childrenMap.get(entityId) ?? []\n    const children = childIds.map(id => buildSvgNode(id, depth + 1))\n    const node: SvgNode = {\n      id: entityId,\n      label,\n      kind,\n      x: 0,\n      y: PADDING + depth * (NODE_H + V_GAP),\n      depth,\n      children,\n    }\n    if (children.length === 0) {\n      node.x = xCounter\n      xCounter += NODE_W + H_GAP\n    } else {\n      const first = children[0]\n      const last = children[children.length - 1]\n      node.x = (first.x + last.x + NODE_W) / 2 - NODE_W / 2\n    }\n    return node\n  }\n\n  const roots = graph.entities.filter(e => !e.parent_id || e.kind === 'repository')\n  const svgRoots = roots.map(r => buildSvgNode(r.id, 0))\n\n  function flattenSvg(nodes: SvgNode[]): SvgNode[] {\n    const result: SvgNode[] = []\n    function visit(n: SvgNode) { result.push(n); n.children.forEach(visit) }\n    nodes.forEach(visit)\n    return result\n  }\n\n  const allSvgNodes = flattenSvg(svgRoots)\n  const svgW = Math.max(600, xCounter + PADDING)\n  const maxDepth = allSvgNodes.reduce((m, n) => Math.max(m, n.depth), 0)\n  const svgH = PADDING * 2 + (maxDepth + 1) * (NODE_H + V_GAP)\n\n  const nodeColors: Record<string, string> = {\n    repository: '#C6F84A',\n    directory: '#F3F5F0',\n    file: '#FFFFFF',\n    virtual_group: '#F2F85B',\n  }\n\n  const svgNodes = allSvgNodes.map(n => {\n    const fill = nodeColors[n.kind] ?? '#FFFFFF'\n    const stroke = n.kind === 'repository' ? '#a0d020' : '#DFE5DA'\n    const label = n.label.length > 18 ? `${n.label.slice(0, 16)}…` : n.label\n    return `<g class=\"map-node\" transform=\"translate(${n.x},${n.y})\">\n      <rect width=\"${NODE_W}\" height=\"${NODE_H}\" rx=\"6\" fill=\"${fill}\" stroke=\"${stroke}\" stroke-width=\"1\"/>\n      <text x=\"${NODE_W / 2}\" y=\"${NODE_H / 2 + 4}\" text-anchor=\"middle\" font-size=\"11\" font-family=\"system-ui,sans-serif\" fill=\"#172019\">${escHtml(label)}</text>\n    </g>`\n  }).join('\\n    ')\n\n  const svgEdges = graph.relations\n    .filter(r => r.kind === 'contains')\n    .map(r => {\n      const src = allSvgNodes.find(n => n.id === r.source_id)\n      const tgt = allSvgNodes.find(n => n.id === r.target_id)\n      if (!src || !tgt) return ''\n      return `<line x1=\"${src.x + NODE_W / 2}\" y1=\"${src.y + NODE_H}\" x2=\"${tgt.x + NODE_W / 2}\" y2=\"${tgt.y}\" stroke=\"#DFE5DA\" stroke-width=\"1\"/>`\n    })\n    .join('\\n    ')\n\n  const deferredList = manifest.deferred_capabilities\n    .map(c => `<li>${escHtml(c)}</li>`)\n    .join('\\n          ')\n\n  // Table of files (top 50 only to keep size reasonable)\n  const fileList = graph.entities\n    .filter(e => e.kind === 'file')\n    .slice(0, 50)\n    .map(e => `<tr><td style=\"font-family:monospace;font-size:11px\">${escHtml(e.path ?? e.label)}</td><td style=\"font-size:11px\">${escHtml(e.metadata?.language as string ?? '')}</td></tr>`)\n    .join('\\n')\n\n  return `<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n<meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n<meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width,initial-scale=1\">\n<title>Grepo: ${title} — Structural Map</title>\n<style>\nbody{margin:0;padding:0;font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;background:#FAFBF7;color:#172019;}\nheader{background:#FFFFFF;border-bottom:1px solid #DFE5DA;padding:16px #DFE5DA;border-radius:6px;font-family:monospace;font-size:10px;color:#526057;}\nmain{padding:32px;}\n.card{background:#FFFFFF;border:1px solid Supports:\n *   - Pan/zoom/fit/reset\n *   - Selection sync with tree and source panel\n *   - Accessible list alternative\n *   - Structural HTML export\n *\n * INTEGRATION_SLOT: dependencies.workspace\n * Dependency/import edges are NOT shown here. They will be added\n * when the Dependencies feature module is connected.\n */\n\nimport { useEffect, useState } from 'react'\nimport { useParams } from 'react-router-dom'\nimport { StructureMap } from '../../components/map/StructureMap'\nimport { SourceViewer } from '../../components/source/SourceViewer'\nimport { IntegrationPlaceholder } from '../../components/slots/IntegrationPlaceholder'\nimport { useWorkspace } from '../../contexts/WorkspaceContext'\nimport { getGraph, getAllFiles } from '../../services/v1/api'\nimport {\n  buildExportManifest,\n  downloadStructuralExport,\n} from '../../services/exportService'\nimport type { Graph, GraphEntity } from '../../types/v1'\n\nexport default function MapPage() {\n  const { projectId = '', snapshotId = '' } = useParams<{ projectId: string; snapshotId: string }>()\n  const ws = useWorkspace()\n  const [graph, setGraph] = useState<Graph | null>(ws.graph)\n  const [graphLoading, setGraphLoading] = useState(false)\n  const [graphError, setGraphError] = useState<string | null>(null)\n\n  // Load graph\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!projectId || !snapshotId) return\n    setGraphLoading(true)\n    setGraphError(null)\n    getGraph(projectId, snapshotId)\n      .then(g => {\n        setGraph(g)\n        ws.setGraph(g)\n      })\n      .catch(err => setGraphError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to load graph.'))\n      .finally(() => setGraphLoading(false))\n  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n  }, [projectId, snapshotId])\n\n  function handleSelectEntity(entity: GraphEntity) {\n    ws.selectEntity({\n      entityId: entity.id,\n      kind: entity.kind,\n      path: entity.path ?? entity.label,\n      fileId: entity.file_id,\n    })\n  }\n\n  async function handleExport() {\n    if (!graph || !ws.snapshot) return\n    // Ensure we have file list for the manifest\n    let fileList = ws.files\n    if (fileList.length === 0) {\n      fileList = await getAllFiles(projectId, snapshotId)\n    }\n    const filePage = { schema_version: '1.0' as const, snapshot_id: snapshotId, files: fileList, total: fileList.length, truncated: false }\n    const manifest = buildExportManifest(ws.snapshot, graph, filePage)\n    downloadStructuralExport(ws.snapshot, graph, manifest)\n  }\n\n  // Selected file for source panel\n  const selectedFile = ws.selectedEntity?.fileId\n    ? null}\n              onSelectEntity={handleSelectEntity}\n              onExport={handleExport}\n            />\n          )}\n\n          {!graph && !graphLoading && !graphError && (\n            <div className=\"empty-state\">\n              <div className=\"empty-state__icon\" aria-hidden=\"true\">⊞</div>\n              <h2 className=\"empty-state__title\">No map data</h2>\n              <p className=\"empty-state__body\">\n                Import a repository first, then open the Map.\n              </p>\n            </div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n\n        {/* Source / context panel */}\n        {selectedFile && (\n          <div style={{ width: 420, borderLeft: '1px solid var(--grepo-line)', display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', overflow: 'hidden' }}>\n            <div className=\"context-panel__header\">\n              <h2 className=\"context-panel__title\">Source</h2>\n            </div>\n            <div style={{ flex: 1, overflow: 'hidden', padding: 12 }}>\n              <SourceViewer\n                projectId={projectId}\n                snapshotId={snapshotId}\n                fileId={selectedFile.id}\n                path={selectedFile.path}\n                language={selectedFile.language}\n                lineStart={ws.selectedEntity?.lineRange?.start}\n                lineEnd={ws.selectedEntity?.lineRange?.end}\n              />\n            </div>\n\n            {/* INTEGRATION_SLOT: summaries.context-panel */}\n            <div style={{ padding: '12px', borderTop: '1px solid var(--grepo-line)' }}>\n              <IntegrationPlaceholder\n                slotId=\"summaries.context-panel\"\n                title=\"AI Summaries\"\n                description=\"Integration slot: put the summaries feature here. Not connected yet.\"\n                showDevDisclosure={false}\n              />\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* INTEGRATION_SLOT: dependencies.workspace */}\n      {/* Dependencies overlay will be provided by the Dependencies feature module */}\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n",
